## 0. Position

Mahāmāyā is Epi-Logos's **determinative psychoid form-transcription system**: the integrated symbolic-computational field in which harmonic/cymatic world-condition, elemental/quaternionic constitution, and situated personal or event context can become a determinate 64-fold inscription and can be followed through its transformations as I-Ching, codon, RNA-expression, Tarot, rotational orientation and clock-form.

This is one system. The branches are not six neighbouring symbolic catalogues. They are distinct moments and readings of one computation.

Mahāmāyā is also the #3 culmination of the `1/2/3` Cosmic pole. M1 provides the relational/harmonic parent and quaternionic/topological act; M2 differentiates it as a 72-fold vibrational-correspondential world-condition; M3 crystallises that condition into a discrete, writable, transformable and narratively legible symbolic form. At #4 the resulting cosmic form is nested as Nara, the situated individual/lived context.

The computational relation is recursive rather than merely sequential. Once Nara exists, a person, paśu, event, session, dream, activity or whole narrative can become an input to Mahāmāyā again. Nara supplies the situated thing that Mahāmāyā is intended to transcribe. The M3↔M4 relation is therefore a circuit:

```text
Cosmic 1/2/3 condition
        ↓
Mahāmāyā inscription
        ↓
Nara lived/situated composition
        ↓
entity · event · activity · narrative · oracle
        ↘
          Mahāmāyā re-inscription
              ↓
      transformed archetypal form
              ↓
             Nara
```

### Constitutional refraction

```text
M3   Mahāmāyā         subsystem + canonical Guardian identity
S3   Software Factory standing O:I product reflection
S3′  Chronos          operative Ta-Onta reflection
M3′  epi.deep.m3      deep/playable Mahāmāyā instrument
```

These are related coordinate roles, not aliases. Software Factory is the standing technological reflection of M3's determinative/form-producing character; Chronos is its same-coordinate operative temporal inscription/reflection; M3′ is the deep professional symbolic-genetic clock/transcription instrument through which Mahāmāyā becomes directly inspectable and usable.

### Mark / Aim ground

> **Mahāmāyā is the integrated calculation by which a potential or situated whole becomes a determinate psychoid form with a discrete address, charge, transformation grammar, expressive orientation, archetypal narrative and temporal/world placement.**

The larger Mark is the Epi relation in which the `1/2/3` Cosmic composition and `4/5/0` Personal composition remain two recursive faces of one Current Situated Matheme.

The Aim of this matrix is to recover enough of that integrated computation that future kernel, provider, renderer, Agent and workbench development can be tested against the intended whole rather than optimising one representation in isolation.

Recognition is achieved when a human or Agent can follow one thing through the system without losing provenance:

```text
thing / event / current world-condition
→ elemental / bioquaternionic constitution
→ 4-state nucleotide articulation
→ 64-address inscription
→ charge + matrix + rotational determination
→ I-Ching transformation
→ DNA/RNA expression
→ Tarot archetypal/narrative form
→ 360°/720° world-clock position
→ Nara lived/developmental return
```

Where the current kernel does not yet complete a step in that chain, this matrix marks the gap rather than inventing the missing law.

### Current human-ratified interpretation incorporated in this matrix

The August 23, 2026 review establishes the following current authored positions for this matrix:

1. M3 must be read as a **single integrated deterministic transcription system**, not a loose cross-reference layer.
2. The computational relation permits `Nara → Mahāmāyā`; M4 supplies a situated entity/event/narrative for M3 to compute.
3. Elemental/bioquaternionic form and the L2′ Alchemical-Elemental aperture are part of the intended route into the nucleotide fourfold.
4. Tarot is an archetypal narrative language capable of encapsulating a whole entity, event, session or developmental arc; its sequence can become a transcription of real beings and situations.
5. Tarot rotational states refine the classical upright/reversed binary into an eightfold potential for greater expressive nuance; non-dual M3 forms retain seven distinct states where the algebra collapses one orientation.
6. Nara is intended to retain a full Tarot deck/session trajectory with shuffle/draw operation, while I-Ching throws track transformations/change across the session.
7. The `22+2` Tarot governance relation is read through **The Fool / The World as the start/finish 0/1 pair carrying additional start/stop governance roles**, rather than requiring two additional physical deck identities.
8. The original 16 clock lenses remain the relatively settled static aperture matrix; the Fibonacci/Pisano field is a pre-lensic/base ground, not a seventeenth static lens.
9. The exact relation between `tick`, `pulse`, two-phase sixfold superposition and later agent-introduced `walk` language remains open for further specification.

The September 1, 2026 physical-pole integration sharpens the same recovered system:

10. The four material elemental carriers continue through the M2→M3 handoff: `72=4×18` becomes `64=4×16`; the epogdoon is therefore readable fibrewise as `18→16` while angular aperture expands `20°→22.5°`.
11. M3's existing polarity×mobility binary can be given direct spatial form as a crease/fold state grammar. Three two-bit articulated sites therefore realise the same six-bit/64-state address field already shared by codon and hexagram.
12. The 64 / 384 / 472 structures can be expressed as **motif / elementary form-change / expressive pose** faces of one Mahāmāyā state. The clock-wheel and codon annulus remain important inspectors, but they need not be the sole rūpa of the system.

### 2.1 What changed against the live map

Compared with the matrix of early September 2026 (which tracked twelve coordinates by name and carried everything below them as counts):

- **Renamed (11 of the 12 tracked coordinates).** 1 (M3-3-2 Matrix Operators) is unchanged; the other 11 differ in wording; none was removed. The pairs are in the table above and the HISTORICAL list in §2.4.
- **Added, as nodes.** 993 live nodes (M3 and M3′ among them) were not tracked as coordinates; the matrix carried what lay below as counts or not at all: 64 Hexagram and 8 Trigram; 4 Nucleotide, 16 NucleotidePair, 64 Codon; 3 MatrixOperator with 192 GenerationEvent (the 192 generation cells); 7 RNAElement and 37 RNACodon; 24 AminoAcid; 2 ChromosomeContainer, 24 ChromosomeElement and the mitochondrial genome; 4 suits with 56 MinorArcanaCard; the Major Arcana node with 22 cards; the phase space with 4 CodonFamily and 64 CodonReflection; 4 CompassAnchor and 24 TranscendentAnchor (the 24 governors); the Axis Mundi, Alpha-Omega and 359 Degree nodes. The classifiers M3-3-0 and M3-3-1 carry six Classifier nodes.
- **Added, as description.** M3′ now carries the owner's 2026-10-20 description; the internal six-fold (q_0–q_5) is carried by seven nodes (§2.2).
- **Shifted roles.** M3-4 is named *Minor Arcana* but also holds the phase space (M3-4.0) and the Major Arcana (M3-4-(5/0)). M3-5 is the *Wheel*; its 24 anchor children are the quadrant palindromes and carry no hexagram. M3-3-0 and M3-3-1 are Classifier nodes that classify the 192 generation cells (48 non-dual: 12 perfect palindromic, 12 imperfect, 24 non-palindromic; 144 dual), not parents of codons. M3-0 is a leaf.
- **Counts.** The matrix quoted about 996 nodes and 4,891 relations from the canonical source. The live map holds 1,005 nodes under the M3 prefix and 5,131 relations touching it (4,664 within it).
- **Relations measured this pass.** 383 LINE_CHANGE relations against the 384 of the law, of which 308 are exact one-line adjacencies (observation O4).

## 3. The integrated Mahāmāyā computation

The recovered whole is best represented as a recursive transduction rather than as a branch list:

```text
M1 harmonic / topological act
            +
M2 72-fold vibrational-correspondential condition
            +
situated Nara/entity/event/activity evidence where present
            │
            ▼
ELEMENTAL / BIOQUATERNIONIC FORM
Earth · Water · Air · Fire / four causal-material qualities
            │
     L2′ Alchemical-Elemental
     reading / material aperture
            │
            ▼
FOUR-STATE NUCLEOTIDE LANGUAGE
A / T / C / G = polarity × mobility
            │
        4 × 4 pair field
            │
sum/difference + three matrix axes
            │
            ▼
FOUR-CHARGE QUATERNION
pp · mm · mp · pm
            │
            ▼
64-ADDRESS INSCRIPTION
codon ↔ hexagram
            │
     ┌──────┼─────────┐
     │      │         │
 complement move   resonance
     │      │         │
 line-change graph    │
     └──────┼─────────┘
            │
            ▼
ROTATIONAL / ORIENTED EXPRESSION
7 or 8 distinct states
            │
            ▼
DNA → RNA TRANSCRIPTION
storage → expression / governance
            │
            ▼
TAROT ARCHETYPAL FORM
card · orientation · sequence · narrative
            │
decan · planet · body-zone · medicine · amino / backbone
            │
            ▼
360° / 720° WORLD-CLOCK
inscription-circle × lens-circle
co-foliated with harmonic K²
            │
            ▼
NARA LIVED / DEVELOPMENTAL CONTEXT
            │
            └─────────────↺ can become new M3 input
```

This diagram contains both **landed paths** and the **intended whole**. The matrix distinguishes them below.

### Current executable direction

The present kernel can:

- receive M2 active evidence and produce an M3 active-mask;
- derive/hold a codon/hexagram address from current degree/lens-mode paths;
- compute the codon's four-charge quaternion;
- compose ring × element × matrix-axis environment;
- choose the active rotational state of each active codon deterministically;
- derive I-Ching transformations, Tarot/codon mappings and degree/transcription facts.

### Missing central inverse seam

The present kernel does **not** yet expose one canonical operation of the form:

```text
Q_entity / Q_composed
      → primary nucleotide/codon/address64
```

It can compute `codon → q` and `environment × codon → active rotation`; the surrounding Nara/M3 architecture intends a real entity/situation to be transcribed into M3 form. The inverse/selection law therefore remains a first-class kernel design gap.

It must be solved from the existing elemental, L2′, M2 evidence, charge and M3 operator relations rather than filled by nearest-neighbour convenience without ratification.

---

## 4. M3-0 — reception, cymatic crystallisation and the 72→64 threshold

M3-0 is the aperture at which a vibrational/correspondential field becomes discrete inscription.

```text
M1 relational/harmonic excitation
        ↓
M2 Vimarśā / audio + nodal field
        ↓
M2 material/cymatic response
        ↓
M2-5 DET evidence / 72-space source
        ↓ 9:8
M3-0 64-space symbolic reception
```

The current M2/M3 code carries both a 72→64 projection table and the integer epogdoon map:

```text
floor(index72 × 8 / 9)
```

This map covers all 64 target addresses. There are **8 codomain collisions** because 72 source positions compress into 64 target positions. A source-index round-trip test can mark **9 source indices as non-closing/fold points**. Those are two descriptions of the many-to-one map and must not be collapsed into one “gap count”.

The older vendor `M3_RES_MATRIX` carried 8 "resonance gaps" in 64-space. It was not the Same-Quality matrix and the native kernel no longer reads it (19 Oct 2026); there is no gap set to relate to the DET collisions.

### Cymatic relation

M2 owns generation of the standing-wave/material evidence. M3 receives the evidence as determinative input and binds it to symbolic form.

This is not a loose visual correspondence:

```text
frequency / mode / nodal relation
        ↓ material response
cymatic form
        ↓ DET / 9:8 threshold
symbolic address + charge + orientation
```

The M2 cymatic and M3 clock are therefore successive expressions of one 1-2-3 computation.

### Element-preserving fibre law

The M2 implementation already admits a four-material-element factorisation of the 72 field through its decan organisation:

```text
72 = 4 × 18
```

Mahāmāyā's own material alphabet gives:

```text
64 = 4 × 16
```

so the epogdoon can be followed inside each material carrier:

```text
4 × 18  --8/9-->  4 × 16
18      --8/9-->  16
```

The conjugate angular read is:

```text
360°/18 = 20°
360°/16 = 22.5°
20° × 9/8 = 22.5°
9×20° = 8×22.5° = 180°
```

Earth, Fire, Water and Air therefore need not be reintroduced at M3 as a second symbolism. They are the material carriers through which the M2 vibrational field is rendered determinate in M3.

At distributed-field grain the same relation can be represented as:

```text
V72 = V18(E) ⊕ V18(F) ⊕ V18(W) ⊕ V18(A)

T72→64 = I4 ⊗ T18→16

R64 = R16(E) ⊕ R16(F) ⊕ R16(W) ⊕ R16(A)
```

The existing integer DET is the current discrete-address form of this passage. A richer M2 modal/cymatic state can preserve amplitudes and modal disposition into M3 form potential before a primary address is resolved.
---

## 5. M3-1 — I-Ching as transformation grammar

The I-Ching gives M3 its native language of **change inside a determinate six-bit form**.

### Landed invariants

```text
64 hexagrams
8 upper/lower trigrams
6 lines per hexagram
64 × 6 = 384 single-line change operators
```

Current operators include:

- `m3_line_change(hex, line) = hex XOR (1 << line)`;
- full complement `hex XOR 0x3F` (an I Ching relation; Matrix 1 acts on a hexagram with the same result by flipping all three lines of each trigram, but on a codon Matrix 1 is the base partner `⊕0x15`);
- upper/lower trigram decomposition and recomposition;
- upper↔lower trigram exchange (an I Ching relation, not a matrix);
- nuclear trigram structure;
- Earlier-Heaven / Later-Heaven positional data.

A live I-Ching cast adds the moving-line mask. The resulting hexagram is not a preselected successor but a deterministic application of the cast's moving lines to the primary hexagram.

### Function inside the integrated system

For an entity/event already transcribed into M3 form:

```text
hexagram = present structural state
moving lines = activated transformation
resulting hexagram = becoming-state
```

This is why the I-Ching is especially suited to Nara's **tracking of transformations**. A Nara session can contain real I-Ching throws whose primary/moving/result structure becomes a time-stamped transformation trace over the same Mahāmāyā substrate.

Randomness/casting chooses the encounter input; the transformation law applied to that input remains deterministic and provenance-bearing.

---

## 6. M3-2 — four-state genetic/computational matter

### The nucleotide fourfold

Current executable law:

| Nucleotide | Bits | Polarity | Mobility | I-Ching value | Tarot suit | Element |
|---|---:|---|---|---:|---|---|
| A | `00` | Yin | Moving | 6 | Cups | Water |
| T | `01` | Yang | Moving | 9 | Wands | Fire |
| C | `10` | Yin | Resting | 8 | Pentacles | Earth |
| G | `11` | Yang | Resting | 7 | Swords | Air |

This gives one compact four-state alphabet from two binary distinctions. A codon is three such two-bit symbols:

```text
4³ = 64
3 nucleotide positions × 2 bits = 6-bit address
```

The same 64-address is read as codon and hexagram. The project is actively developing the deeper I-Ching/DNA identity of this shared form; the matrix treats the common address and transformations as established implementation facts and the full psychoid/physical significance as an active research programme.

### L2′ / elemental relation

The current authored integration places the **L2′ Alchemical-Elemental** aperture at the important transition between an entity/world's elemental constitution and its Mahāmāyā transcription.

The intended relation is:

```text
elemental / bioquaternionic constitution
        ↓ L2′
qualitative material reading
        ↓
A/T/C/G four-state articulation
```

The exact deterministic inverse from a continuous or composed elemental quaternion to the primary nucleotide/codon address is not yet a settled kernel law.

### The 16 dinucleotide relations

The 4×4 pair field has four homogeneous pairs shared across all three matrix families plus twelve differentiated pairs:

```text
shared: AA TT CC GG

Matrix 1 / complementary:
AT TA CG GC

Matrix 2 / moving-resting:
AG GA TC CT

Matrix 3 / same-quality / cross-diagonal:
AC CA TG GT
```

Each pair carries a dataset-native `(sum_value, difference_value)`.

Current Matrix-2 difference values are deliberately **class-stable** (`AG/GA = +2`, `TC/CT = -2`) rather than a simple ordered subtraction. The exact semantic definition of `difference_value` therefore remains to be stated more rigorously at source level.

### Three matrix axes

The C kernel assigns the three pair-matrix families directly to quaternion axes:

```text
Matrix 1 → i
Matrix 2 → j
Matrix 3 → k
```

At 64-space the three matrices act as the base partner at every site (corrected
19 Oct 2026; the earlier table read full six-line inversion, trigram exchange and
"56 admitted states + 8 gaps", none of which is a matrix):

```text
Matrix 1 Complementary  → A↔T, C↔G   codon ⊕0x15
Matrix 2 Moving/Resting → A↔G, T↔C   codon ⊕0x3F
Matrix 3 Same-Quality   → A↔C, T↔G   codon ⊕0x2A
```

Each is total (no gaps); Matrix 1 then Matrix 2 is Matrix 3.

The later Clifford work proposes a deeper relation to the three Clifford involutions. That mapping remains a research proposition requiring exact verification against the M3 operator laws.

### Direct fold / rūpa grammar

The same two distinctions can be carried by a spatially articulated fold site:

```text
polarity  = signed crease orientation / valley-mountain state
mobility  = moving-resting hinge state
```

Under a fixed orientation convention these are the same two bits already carried by the nucleotide:

```text
Yin  + Moving   → A / Water
Yang + Moving   → T / Fire
Yin  + Resting  → C / Earth
Yang + Resting  → G / Air
```

Three articulated sites therefore give:

```text
3 sites × 2 bits = 6 bits
4³ = 2⁶ = 64 canonical fold/rūpa motifs
```

A one-bit change in one site is the spatial-form enactment of an M3 line change. The full local adjacency remains:

```text
64 motifs × 6 one-bit changes = 384 elementary form transformations
```

The `4×4=16` dinucleotide field supplies a natural intermediate geometry. If each ordered pair is given a `22.5°` angular address, a codon `XYZ` can be expressed through the two overlapping pair relations `XY` and `YZ`, sharing `Y` as the common hinge/mediating state. This provides a generated form grammar rather than assigning an arbitrary sculpture to each codon.

The three existing matrix operations then act on the same form along the already-landed quaternion axes `i/j/k`. Symbol transformation, quaternion-axis operation and visible fold transformation can therefore be three concomitant registers of one M3 operation.

---

## 7. Four-charge accounting, quaternion form and the 360 invariant

Every codon is evaluated by the four closed-form charges:

```text
pp = X + Y + Z
mm = X - Y - Z
mp = X - Y + Z
pm = X + Y - Z
```

where `X/Y/Z` are the I-Ching values of the codon's outer/middle/inner nucleotides.

The current kernel maps this exactly to a quaternion:

```text
q_codon = (pp, mm, mp, pm)
```

and preserves the identity:

```text
pp + mm + mp + pm = 4X
```

### 360 integral

Summing raw `pp` over all 64 codons yields:

```text
1440
```

The system's normalised integral is `/4`:

```text
1440 / 4 = 360
```

Per suit / outer-nucleotide family:

```text
A / Cups       336 / 4 = 84
T / Wands      384 / 4 = 96
C / Pentacles  368 / 4 = 92
G / Swords     352 / 4 = 88
                         ───
                         360
```

This is an exact **internal M3 computational invariant** and a primary reason the genetic/Tarot charge system closes onto the 360° world-clock.

It is therefore more than a decorative correspondence: the full four-family charge accounting supplies the clock's 360 total in the system's own arithmetic.

---

## 8. M3-3 — rotational composition and the Universal Transcription Engine

### 40 / 24 codon classes

Current production classification:

```text
4   perfect palindromic
12  imperfect palindromic
24  non-palindromic non-dual
24  dual
──────────────────────────
64

40 non-dual × 7 states = 280
24 dual     × 8 states = 192
                           ───
                           472
```

`TCT / Nine of Wands` is currently classified as a seven-state non-dual form by code/spec/tests; stale dataset material that says eight is reconciliation work, not current authority.

### Why there are rotational states

The raw eightfold generation comes from two directed ways of resolving the two overlapping dinucleotide relations of codon `XYZ`.

Negative family:

```text
XY held
?Z varies
first relation retains authority while second adapts
```

Positive family:

```text
X? varies
YZ held
second relation retains authority while first adapts
```

This yields four negative + four positive candidate orientations. Where internal non-duality collapses one distinct orientation, the realised set has seven.

### Tarot function — eightfold refinement of upright / reversed

The current authored purpose of this rotation field is a **granular Tarot orientation language**.

Classical Tarot ordinarily gives a card a twofold orientation:

```text
upright / reversed
```

Mahāmāyā retains that polarity as the coarse twofold but expands the card's expressive potential into an eight-position field at 45° intervals:

```text
0° · 45° · 90° · 135° · 180° · 225° · 270° · 315°
```

For non-dual forms one position is algebraically non-distinct, leaving seven actual states.

The point is increased expressive resolution: the same card/archetypal form can carry a more exact orientation than the classical upright/reversed binary, allowing a reading to distinguish finer modes of manifestation, tension, inversion, emergence or return.

In the rūpa instrument this same field becomes **posture**: a canonical 64-fold form can occupy its lawful 7/8 expressive orientations. The `472` surface is thereby simultaneously codon-rotation, Tarot orientation and finite pose-space of the determinate form.

### Current deterministic environmental orientation

The kernel already contains a significant part of this intent.

`m3_det_with_quaternion()` composes:

```text
ring quaternion
× active element quaternion
× selected M3 matrix axis
→ composed environment quaternion
```

For every active codon in the DET-produced mask, it then calls:

```text
m3_quat_active_state(composed_environment, codon)
```

to select the active rotational state.

Thus the rotational field is not merely a set of optional display positions. Current code already treats it as **environment-conditioned orientation of an active symbolic form**.

The unresolved part is upstream primary-form selection: given an arbitrary situated `Q_entity` or `Q_composed`, what canonical law chooses the primary codon/address before the environment chooses its orientation?

---

## 9. DNA → RNA → Tarot expression

### RNA as expression phase

The developed transcription research gives RNA a central mediating role between stored symbolic structure and expressed/narrative form.

For the DNA alphabet `{A,T,C,G}`:

```text
total codons            = 4³ = 64
T-free codons           = 3³ = 27
T-containing codons     = 64 - 27 = 37
new U-containing forms  = 37
DNA+RNA unique forms    = 64 + 37 = 101
```

The intended T→U shift is a **storage→expression transcription**: U does not become a fifth address slot; it is the efficient/dynamic position in its transcribed form.

The current dataset rotational protocol already contains an RNA enhancement that creates U-containing rotational descendants from T-containing DNA states.

### Current implementation contradiction

`portal-core/src/mahamaya.rs` currently models `rna_phase` by XOR-flipping the polarity bit of **every** nucleotide. This can transform `CCC` into `GGG`, which is not the later T→U transcription architecture.

Therefore:

```text
CURRENT IMPLEMENTATION: polarity-wide RNA phase toggle
CURRENT DOMAIN/RESEARCH DIRECTION: T→U on T-containing codons, 37 transformed + 27 shared
STATUS: unresolved contradiction / implementation reconciliation required
```

### Amino / protein layer

The kernel carries codon→amino mappings, start/stop detection, a 24-slot output/backbone vocabulary and an ORF-style transcription path.

The current C/Rust amino tables include simplified/non-standard assignments and project-specific 24-slot extensions. They are valid implementation evidence for the symbolic-protein/backbone mechanism but are not yet a fully reconciled biological mapping authority. Exact biological correspondence should be audited without discarding the intended 24-fold symbolic/output architecture.

---

## 10. M3-4 — Tarot as archetypal and developmental transcription

Tarot is the human/narrative expression face of Mahāmāyā.

A Tarot form can encapsulate:

- an entity;
- a person or paśu;
- a current situation;
- a relationship;
- an event;
- a session;
- a developmental trajectory;
- a transformation sequence.

Because cards are connected to codon, rotation, element, decan, planet, body zone, medicine, amino/backbone and wider graph relations, a Tarot sequence can serve as a **language for transcribing a whole into archetypal form** rather than as an isolated divination result.

### Minor Arcana — 64→56 structural compression

Current C mapping covers all 64 codons through 56 Minor Arcana cards:

```text
48 single-codon cards
8 dual-codon court cards × 2 codons each
= 48 + 16 codon slots
= 64 codons
```

The four suits are the four nucleotide/element families:

```text
Cups       ↔ A ↔ Water
Wands      ↔ T ↔ Fire
Pentacles  ↔ C ↔ Earth
Swords     ↔ G ↔ Air
```

The court dual-codon structure retains relation/orientation rather than forcing a one-card/one-codon reduction.

### Major Arcana and the 22+2 governance relation

Current code materialises 22 Major Arcana mapped to chromosome-pair / amino-output indices.

The current human-ratified interpretation of the additional `+2` is:

```text
The Fool  ↔ start / opening / 0
The World ↔ stop / completion / 1
```

The two governance roles are **additional functional readings of the boundary Major Arcana**, forming the 0/1 start-finish relation of an expressional sequence. They do not require two extra physical Tarot cards beyond the 78-card deck.

Current code/spec language that calls these “2 transcendent operators” should be aligned to this relation during later kernel/spec reconciliation.

### Nara session/development tracking

The intended Nara session experience includes a **full Tarot deck** whose draw/shuffle history can follow the movement of a session.

A session can therefore produce:

```text
ordered card sequence
+ card orientation / M3 rotational state
+ NOW/tick/world-condition at each draw
+ question/position/lens context
+ whole-reading synthesis
→ archetypal trajectory of the session
```

Across sessions, these trajectories can become developmental narratives for the same entity.

Current Nara oracle artifacts already preserve ordered card draws, position, upright/reversed orientation, three-level interpretation, whole-reading synthesis, M3 references and DAY/NOW/session lineage. The missing integration is to carry the full M3 7/8 rotational state instead of only the classical twofold orientation, and to materialise the full-deck/session trajectory as a first-class live instrument rather than only individual oracle artifacts.

### I-Ching complement in Nara

Tarot primarily supplies **archetypal entity/narrative expression**.

I-Ching primarily supplies **transformation/change articulation** through moving-line throws.

They share the same M3 address field, so a Nara session can track both:

```text
Tarot sequence → what whole/archetypal story is moving
I-Ching throws → how the current form is transforming
```

Random shuffle/throw is the encounter/casting mechanism; canonical M3 computation supplies the deterministic structure and transformation once the cast is known.

---

## 11. M3-5 — the clock as totalised symbolic-cosmic form

M3-5 is the head/totalisation of the `1/2/3` physical/cosmic pole: the full cosmic articulation before and during its nesting in the individual.

### 360° / 720°

The 360° field is supported internally by the charge integral and the current `CLOCK_DEGREE_LUT[360]`.

The 720° field retains the SU(2) double cover:

```text
first 360°  = primary / bimba phase
second 360° = conjugate / pratibimba phase
full identity return requires 720°
```

Current implementation also retains a 24-spoke/backbone structure at 15° intervals, zodiac/sign/decan relations, and line-change/hexagram addressability across the degree field.

### The 16 static lenses

The relatively settled original 16-lens matrix partitions the same 360° field through reciprocal factor-pair apertures:

```text
0   1° × 360
1   2° × 180
2   4° × 90
3   8° × 45

4   9° × 40
5  10° × 36
6  12° × 30
7  15° × 24

8  24° × 15
9  30° × 12
10 36° × 10
11 40° × 9

12 45° × 8
13 90° × 4
14 180° × 2
15 360° × 1
```

Reciprocal pairs:

```text
0↔15  1↔14  2↔13  3↔12
4↔11  5↔10  6↔9   7↔8
```

A degree therefore carries multiple simultaneous partition readings. The lens changes the resolution/aperture through which the same inscription is understood; it does not create another clock.

### Fibonacci/Pisano ground

The later 60-position Fibonacci/Pisano work is best retained as a **pre-lensic/base synchronisation field**:

```text
60 × 6° = 360°
```

It is not a seventeenth static lens. Its exact deeper derivation and relation to the sixteen lenses remains active specification work.

### 16+1 fold-aperture composition

The sixteen static apertures can additionally be given a **meta-orientation** over the same 360° form field at the M3 sixteenfold quantum:

```text
16 × 22.5° = 360°
```

This does not replace the divisor geometry of any aperture. It gives the sixteen apertures a common orientation ring over the Fibonacci/Pisano base. The eight reciprocal pairs can occupy opposed meta-orientations, making aperture reciprocity physically expressible as a conjugate fold across the same living form.

The three relevant angular grids then synchronize as:

```text
Fibonacci/Pisano ground:  6°
M2 elemental fibre:      20°
M3 form aperture:        22.5°

lcm(6°,20°)    = 60°  → 6 closures around 360°
lcm(6°,22.5°)  = 90°  → 4 closures around 360°
lcm(20°,22.5°) = 180° → 2 closures around 360°
```

The combined field therefore exposes a `6 / 4 / 2` synchronization; the M3-touching closures make the `4+2` structure directly visible inside the shared physical-pole clock geometry.

### Tick / pulse / diachronic relation — open

Some later architecture labels a 12-fold diachronic traversal a `walk`. That terminology is not ratified here as the final semantics of Spanda.

The current authored question is richer:

```text
diachronic:
    12-fold progression can be read as a traversal

synchronic:
    two-phase superposition of a sixfold state

within each sixfold:
    two superposed four-beat runs
    held by two defining poles / start-finish conditions
```

The matrix therefore preserves `tick12`, 12-fold double-cover/Spanda evidence and existing traversal implementations, while leaving the exact pulse/walk ontology open for further work.

### Two orthogonal wheels / Mahāmāyā torus

The current canonical geometric successor to the earlier “two orthogonal wheels” conception is:

```text
T²_Mahāmāyā
=
inscription-circle × lens-circle
```

These are two independent cyclic degrees of freedom:

- **inscription circle** — which form / degree / symbolic configuration is present;
- **lens circle** — through which partition/aperture the same form is being read.

Their product is toroidal because the two cycles are independent and intersect at a current reading.

The full M3-5 world-clock is then:

```text
K² × T²_Mahāmāyā

K²:
    M1/M2 harmonic-audio-genesis double-cover

T²_Mahāmāyā:
    inscription × lens symbolic-transcription torus
```

This is the most complete present statement of the relation among music, cymatics and the clock:

```text
M1 ratio/pulse/topology
→ M2 vibration/material/cymatic form
→ M3 inscription/orientation
→ M3-5 temporal-lensic world form
```

Sound, standing-wave and symbolic-clock state are successive determinations of one cosmic event.

## 12. M3′ — professional deep instrument

M3′ is the deep playable/inspectable form of this system.

Its principal experience should be a **professional symbolic-genetic clock/transcription instrument** in which one current inscription can be read across all of its lawful forms without renderer-local reinvention.

The September physical-pole integration strengthens that experience into a **living rūpa instrument**: the currently selected/derived M3 address can inhabit a generated fold-body whose motif, six one-bit neighbours, matrix transformation, 7/8-state pose, Tarot expression and clock/aperture relation are all projections of the same underlying state.

### Human experience

A person should be able to:

- select or receive the current cosmic inscription;
- bring a person/entity/event/session/narrative into the M3 relation where a canonical transcriber exists;
- inspect the elemental/quaternionic evidence that contributed to the inscription;
- see the active codon/hexagram and its four charges;
- see the same address as its canonical fold/rūpa motif;
- traverse its six elementary one-bit/form-change neighbours and see the I-Ching line-change identity of each move;
- see why its 7/8 rotational orientation is active and experience that orientation as the pose of the same form;
- follow moving-line transformations;
- follow DNA→RNA→amino / expression state;
- read the same form as Tarot, including granular orientation;
- inspect decanic, planetary, body-zone, medicine and backbone relations from canonical graph/source data;
- track a Tarot narrative sequence and I-Ching transformation sequence through a Nara session;
- view the 360°/720° clock, Fibonacci base and current lens aperture;
- move among living-rūpa, flat/debug, lens-annulus, toroidal/world-clock and Hopf/identity depth views while remaining on the same underlying state;
- inspect source/provenance/readiness for every derived relation.

The deterministic engine chooses/transcribes state from actual inputs. Human interaction selects evidence, context, reading depth, casting events and inspectable alternatives; it does not replace the computation with arbitrary symbolic manipulation.

### Agent experience

An Agent should receive the same semantic refs and state objects as the human surface and be able to:

- resolve M3 addresses and source relations;
- request deterministic transcription/rotation/change operations;
- resolve `address64 ↔ six-bit fold motif ↔ codon/hexagram` without renderer-local tables;
- explain derivation and provenance;
- traverse canonical Tarot/I-Ching/codon/RNA/clock relations;
- consume Nara session trajectory handles without reading protected bodies unless capability permits;
- create structured readings/inspections that remain attached to current event/entity refs;
- distinguish current implementation, design commitment, research proposition and unresolved decision.

### Current profile-bus gap

The current `MathemeHarmonicProfile` exposes:

- tick / degree360 / degree720;
- `lens_mode` (12×7 MEF/M1′ playing landscape);
- `audio_octet`;
- `nodal_quartet`;
- M2 resonance/planetary fields;
- Mahāmāyā binary/address projection;
- codon-rotation projection;
- `q_cosmic`.

It does **not** yet expose the M3 16-lens + Fibonacci-ground aperture state as a first-class `m3_lens_stack` / equivalent field, nor the full typed `TranscriptionalClockPacket`, nor a typed fold/rūpa form-state projection. These are concrete profile-bus development gaps.

## 12A. M3′ in the DLW — where each coordinate lives

*Added 2026-10-20.* Owner rulings of 20 October 2026, and the live map where they are recorded on it. This section fills the protocol's *M′ lived/deep operation* and *current O:I native owners* fields for every coordinate. Shell evidence is from `/Users/admin/Central/worktrees/env-1/o-i` (branch `dlw/build`, HEAD moved from `1519f39d7` to `9a6d282e3` during the read; the working tree was dirty in files this section does not cite), under `packages/live-shell/ui/src`; evidence from elsewhere in that tree is tagged *adjacent*. The DLW manual is the scratch copy of `docs/dlw/DLW.md`.

### 12A.1 The rulings

- **Six app layers.** O:I holds six app layers: Live holds M1′, M2′ and M3′ played as one instrument; Expressions is M4′ (the lived Expression, your own occasion); Technē is M5′; M0′ is the graph systems and the planet (O-I main f78bc6aa4, DLW manual §25.1).
- **One instrument per subsystem.** Each subsystem is one instrument, "the device as a whole is encapsulated in the 0/5 layers"; "the 0/5 can be the full form with the 1-4 as a subrack" (Live's rack UI), recursively, as deep as the map goes.
- **Folding.** "other ql devices should fold into this ontology, chakras go into M4 as the outcome of the M4.0 identity matrix layers (bioquaternion to chakra mapping, M4->M3->M2), devices in their functional relations express the coordinate system as functionality/techne".
- **The elemental carrier.** M4 matrix §1 points 12–14: the elemental quaternion basis [Earth, Fire, Water, Air] is a continuous carrier from M2 material fibres through M3 nucleotide/form families into M4; Q_composed = Q_identity · Q_transit · Q_activity; the bioquaternion is a bimba/pratibimba reading of that composition.

The live map now records the layers (owner ruling 2026-10-20, `promotion_source: claude-code:agency-ground`):

| Node | Live name | Live description |
|---|---|---|
| M0′ | Anuttara′ | The app layer of M0 Anuttara: graph systems (the Bimba map and the knowledge graphs drawn as ground) and the planetary continuity between local and collective, a person's own World joined to the shared field. |
| M1′ | Paramasiva′ | The app layer of M1 Paramaśiva: generation. The torus is the only source of sound; Live plays it with M2′ and M3′. |

| M4′ | Nara′ | The app layer of M4 Nara: Expressions, the lived application, where a scene holds M1′–M3′ with a person's own occasion. |
| M5′ | Epii′ | The app layer of M5 Epii: Technē, the deep-working application of knowledge, construction and review. It contains the Backend Studio (M5-2′), the Frontend Studio (M5-3′) and the Agentic Control Room (M5-4′). |

**Reading the columns.** *Standing* is `DESIGN-COMMITMENT` for today's placement (the owner rulings above and the manual); `IMPLEMENTATION-FACT` is added only where the shell, or the cradle files it depends on, shows the thing, and the evidence names the file. *Readiness* is the protocol's vocabulary and is the readiness of the first-named carrier; the gaps of the other carriers are written in the row's note. No row is marked `ALREADY-REAL` on the strength of a design or a binder alone: the protocol (§8) reserves it for a proving composition, and this refresh read no acceptance receipt. The two constitutional-naming rows (M3-C35, M4-C61) are the exception, because the fact is the name recorded on the live node. `NATIVE-GAP` is read from the catalogue, the family manifest and the binders; the protocol asks that it be returned by an execution before native work is made, so each is a candidate until then.

### 12A.4 Row detail

**P3-00 — The M3′ instrument as a whole.** *Lived / deep operation:* Play the Mahāmāyā form as one instrument: the clock is the face of one current inscription (0/5); the Deck, the Form matrix and Binding are its subrack (1–4), giving the same state its I-Ching, codon, transcription and Tarot faces; the Resonator bank sounds the clock's cut. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (ql.m3-state/v1) through the O:I kernel op m3_reception; the Live chain and the catalogue designs (O:I shell). *Evidence:* [live-map] node M3′ (Mahamaya′): 'The app layer of M3 Mahāmāyā: form and time. The codons, the clock and the deck; part of Live.' promotion_source: owner ruling 2026-10-20.; [manual] §25.1: M1-2-3′ is played by Live as one instrument; M3′ closes the pattern into form: the 64 codons, the clock of 360 degrees and its 24 fixed points, and the deck.; [shell] no rack or subrack device composes these five; the QL family manifest declares the faces singly (quaternalLogicFamilyManifest.ts:51-111); Resonator bank and Binding are not faces of it. *Note:* Rack composition only. The pattern recurs inside the map: M3-3 (0 Non-Dual Codon Relations … 5 Human Karyotype, 1–4 between), M3-4 (0 = M3-4.0 phase space, 1–4 = four suits, 5/0 = Major Arcana), M3-5 (5/0 = centre, 1–4 = four anchors). NATIVE-GAP here is read from the catalogue and manifest; the protocol asks that it be returned by an execution before native work is made.

**P3-01 — Reception: the 0 of the form.** *Lived / deep operation:* Receive the M1 quaternion and the M2 vibrational, planetary and elemental state and see it become one 64-address inscription: the 72→64 threshold with its collisions drawn, not hidden. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (ql.m3-state/v1; kernel op m3_reception, oi.m3-reception-context/v1); the 9:8 compression is the kernel's (Binding's Nonets). *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-0 (leaf): TRANSFORMS_72_TO_64_VIA M2-5, RECEIVES_VIBRATIONAL_MATRIX_FROM M2, INHERITS_QUATERNION_FROM M1-5, ANCHORS_SYNTHESIS_WHEEL M3-5; M3-5 RETURNS_TO M3-0.; [shell] the clock binder reads the form through m3_reception (qlM3Clock.ts:1-9); Binding's Nonets exist in the design only (quaternal-logic__binding.tsx:9, 141).; [design-doc] APERTURES-AND-CLOCK-CENTRE.md §3-4: M3-0 is the field of reception and differentiation, realised centrally in M3-5-(5/0). *Note:* Clock reception is bound; the Binding half has no owner (NATIVE-GAP) and a typed 72→64 transduction packet is still an EPI-GAP in the readiness ledger (M3P-TRANSDUCTION, 2026-09-03).

**P3-02 — The wheel: the 5 of the form.** *Lived / deep operation:* Read where the current inscription stands on the 360° wheel and its 720° double cover, cut by a lens, opened into its parts while the subject stays. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (ql.m3-state/v1; m3_reception); the view's own choices (Turn, Open, Readout, Reciprocal) have no owner by design. *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-5 The 360° Mythic Synthesis Wheel: six-fold q_0…q_5; c_3_lens_system '16 archetypal divisions from 1°×360 to 360°×1'; c_1_chamber_structure '9 chambers × 40°'; q_2 the 720° double covering.; [shell] quaternal-logic__m3-clock.tsx and qlM3Clock.ts: wheel with 24 governors of 15°, 9 chambers of 40°, cut spokes, dashed reciprocal, needle; readings clock.degree720, clock.tick12, clock.backbone, aperture.index, form.address, form.codon. *Note:* The clock is bound to the person's own, private M3 form; with no identity chosen, no Expression open or no form opened, every value stands absent and says so (qlM3Clock.ts:12-14).

**P3-03 — Centre and degrees.** *Lived / deep operation:* Stand at a degree, turn the wheel through its shadow, and return to the centre that is both origin and completion. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (m3_reception advance-clock{steps}); the centre is drawn, not operated. *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-5-(5/0) Axis Mundi — Central Unity Origin (c_0_dual_nature 'QuintessentialUnity AND ZeroDegree'); M3-5-(5/0)-(0/360) Alpha-Omega Point; M3-5-(5/0)-1…359 Degree nodes (359) with yin-yang balance, elemental affinity, quadrant 1–4 and rotational phase; 360 positions in all.; [shell] the needle and degree dial are real; the centre is a dot (m3-clock.tsx:103); no operation implements the live node's 'instantaneous translation between any two positions'. *Note:* Boundary convention differs: live degree nodes put degree 90 in quadrant 1 (quadrants 1–90, 91–180, 181–270, 271–359+0); the shell computes floor(degree/90). Recorded as D3.

**P3-04 — Compass anchors and the 24 governors.** *Lived / deep operation:* Name the governor the needle stands in: the first source of the position's reading. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (ql.m3-state/v1 backbone ref). *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-5-1…4 CompassAnchor (North, South, East, West) with six TranscendentAnchor children each (24 nodes): the children are perfect palindromes GGG (quadrant 1, Winter), AAA (2, Spring), CCC (3, Summer), TTT (4, Autumn); none carries a hexagram.; [shell] m3-clock.tsx:52 parses '#3-5-([1-4])-([0-5])' and computes governor = (q−1)·6 + i; the governor's hexagram and codon come from the kernel, not from these nodes. *Note:* Parent anchors run North, South, East, West (M3-5-1…4) while their children's quadrants and seasons run Winter, Spring, Summer, Autumn (clockwise) and the shell reads q clockwise. Recorded as D1; not adjudicated.

**P3-05 — Lenses, chambers, Fibonacci ground, void ring (properties of M3-5).** *Lived / deep operation:* Re-read one inscription at a chosen resolution and hear the same cut as a ring of tuned bodies; the cut is one, held by the bank and drawn by the clock. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (pole/aperture.rs STATIC_DIVISION_DEG10; operations set-aperture and reciprocal-aperture). Authored owner of the aperture catalogue: M2-0 (APERTURES-AND-CLOCK-CENTRE.md §1-2). *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-5 text carries '16 division lenses' and '9×40° chambers'; M2-0 has no children in the live map (the 18-aperture architecture is not applied there).; [shell] clock reads aperture.index and draws spokes and the dashed reciprocal; the Resonator bank design is complete but has no owner binder (resonator-bank.tsx:1-23). Fibonacci ground and void ring: not drawn (NATIVE-GAP against the manual). Which coordinate owns the apertures, M2-0 or M3-5, is conflict W6.

**P3-06 — I-Ching: trigrams and hexagrams.** *Lived / deep operation:* Cast, read and follow a hexagram as the present structural state of the form, its moving lines as the activated transformation, the result as the becoming-state. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF oracle journey (kernel op nara_journey, ql.nara-oracle-journey/v1) for casts; QL-MEF M3 state for the form's hexagram. *Evidence:* [live-map] M3-1 I-Ching System: 8 Trigram (M3-1-0…7) × 8 Hexagram (M3-1-t-h, t lower trigram, h upper trigram index) = 64; q_0…q_5 six-fold.; [shell] qlDeck.ts:1-15: iching = the latest hexagram's six lines and its nuclear hexagram, act.cast with consent to chance; clock form.address. *Note:* The Deck's reading acts are also M4.2-2 (see P4-23).

**P3-07 — Line changes (relations of M3-1).** *Lived / deep operation:* Follow a change: one line flips and the codon, the junctions and the cell change with it. *Current O:I native owners:* QL-MEF (m3_line_change = hex XOR (1<<line), authority); the graph's LINE_CHANGE relations are not the authority. *Evidence:* [live-map] 383 LINE_CHANGE relations (not 384). Measured 2026-10-20 from the live graph. *Note:* Neighbour traversal (the 384 one-bit changes as a view) has no device: NATIVE-GAP. The kernel law stands as authority; the live graph's edge set diverges from it and is recorded, not corrected.

### 12A.6 The working placement against the live map

**Held.** The live map supports these parts of the working placement:

- **M3′ is the codons, the clock and the deck, part of Live.** Live node M3′: 'The app layer of M3 Mahāmāyā: form and time. The codons, the clock and the deck; part of Live.'
- **Frame by M3's 0 and 5.** M3-0 ANCHORS_SYNTHESIS_WHEEL → M3-5 and M3-5 RETURNS_TO M3-0; the wheel's centre M3-5-(5/0) is stated as 'QuintessentialUnity AND ZeroDegree'; five DEVELOPS_INTO edges run M3-0 → M3-1 → M3-2 → M3-3 → M3-4 → M3-5.
- **The 360° wheel and its shadow turn.** 360 position nodes (Alpha-Omega M3-5-(5/0)-(0/360) and Degree 1…359); M3-5 q_2 states the 720° double covering.
- **24 governors.** 24 TranscendentAnchor nodes, six under each of four CompassAnchor nodes (content and order: D1).
- **64 generation cells under each matrix.** 192 GenerationEvent nodes, 64 under each of Matrix 1, 2, 3 (M3-3-2-0…2); each RESOLVES_TO a hexagram and is classified (48 non-dual, 144 dual).
- **The M′ matrices' Form layer.** 64 Codon nodes (M3-2-n-p-c) and 64 Hexagram nodes (M3-1-t-h) are the Form matrix's cells.
- **Its 1–4 as a subrack, recursively.** M3-1…M3-4 each carry a six-fold; the pattern recurs at M3-3 (0 Non-Dual … 5 Karyotype), M3-4 (0 = M3-4.0 … 5/0 = Major Arcana) and M3-5 (5/0 centre, 1–4 anchors).

**Conflicts with the working placement.** The live map is recorded as it stands; nothing is adjudicated.

**W6 — Where the apertures live: M3-5 or M2-0.** *Working placement:* The Resonator bank (it sounds the clock's cut) is part of the M3′ instrument; the 16 cuts belong to the wheel. *Live map:* Live M3-5 carries the 16 division lenses and 9 chambers in its own text (c_3_lens_system, c_1_chamber_structure). Live M2-0 has no children: the 18-aperture architecture (M2-0-0, M2-0-1, M2-0-2-0…7) is not applied in the map. *Evidence:* Live query 2026-10-20 (M2-0 prefix returns one node). The authored architecture (Work/Quaternal-Logic/docs/kernel-rebuild/APERTURES-AND-CLOCK-CENTRE.md §1-2) says 'M2-0 owns the complete eighteen-aperture architecture'; the shell panel headers repeat it (quaternal-logic__resonator-bank.tsx:2-4). *Effect on this draft:* The placement is held at M3-5 (as the live node says) and the bank is marked as sounding a cut whose catalogue is authored at M2-0. Not adjudicated.

**W3 — The Deck stands in two instruments.** *Working placement:* The Deck is M3′ form. *Live map:* Agreed by the map: node M3′ reads 'The codons, the clock and the deck'. But the Deck's readings are Nara's oracle journey (M4.2-1…4) and the manual files Journeys under M4.2. *Evidence:* Live M3′ description; qlDeck.ts:1-5 (the owner is QL's oracle journey); manual §24.4, §24.9. *Effect on this draft:* One device, two placements: the form side (cards and hexagrams with their codons) in the M3′ subrack, the reading side (draw, cast, assign, comment, journey) in the M4′ subrack at M4.2.

**Disagreements of the manual or the shell with the live map** (not conflicts with the owner's placement, but they decide what a row may claim):

**D1 — 24 governors: ordering and content.** *Working placement:* (Manual §22.3, not the owner's placement.) Each of the 24 governors names a hexagram and a codon. *Live map:* 24 TranscendentAnchor nodes exist (M3-5-q-i), but each carries only the quadrant's perfect palindrome (GGG, AAA, CCC, TTT) and no hexagram. The parents are ordered North, South, East, West (M3-5-1…4); the children's quadrants and seasons run Winter, Spring, Summer, Autumn. *Evidence:* Live properties: M3-5-2 'South Cardinal Anchor' (Summer_Solstice) with children labelled Spring; M3-5-3 'East' with children labelled Summer. Shell: m3-clock.tsx:52 reads q clockwise (q=2 is degrees 90–179). *Effect on this draft:* The governor ring is placed on the 24 anchor nodes by coordinate; the hexagram and codon per governor are the kernel's, not the graph's. The parent/children order is recorded as an inconsistency of the map.

**D2 — Name of the third matrix.** *Working placement:* (Matrix document and manual §22.3, not the owner's placement.) Matrix 3 is the Same-quality matrix (A↔C, T↔G). *Live map:* M3-3-2-2 'Matrix 3' is described as the Resonance Matrix ('sympathetic vibrations … harmonic conductor'). *Evidence:* Live M3-3-2-2 c_1_description. The matrix document records that the older 'resonance' table was not the Same-Quality matrix (corrected 19 Oct 2026). *Effect on this draft:* Placement follows the three families by order (1, 2, 3); the live wording is recorded.

**D3 — Quadrant boundary convention.** *Working placement:* (Shell, not the owner's placement.) Quadrants of the wheel are 0–89, 90–179, 180–269, 270–359. *Live map:* Live degree nodes assign degree 90 to quadrant 1, 180 to quadrant 2, 270 to quadrant 3 (quadrants 1–90, 91–180, 181–270, 271–359 plus Alpha-Omega 0/360). *Evidence:* Live: M3-5-(5/0)-90 m_3_5_quadrant 1, -91 quadrant 2; shell m3-clock.tsx:60 floor(deg/90). *Effect on this draft:* Recorded; no placement changes.

**D4 — Fibonacci ground and void ring: manual against shell.** *Working placement:* (Manual §22.3.) The wheel carries the Fibonacci ground (60 × 6°) and the void ring (16 × 22.5°). *Live map:* The map is not the disagreeing party here: manual §22.3 says both are drawn and not a cut you choose, and the panel's wheel draws governors, chambers, the cut, its reciprocal and the needle only. *Evidence:* quaternal-logic__m3-clock.tsx:75-113 (no Fibonacci, void or 22.5 in the file). *Effect on this draft:* Marked NATIVE-GAP against the manual (M3-C28, M3-C39).

**D5 — Which chains hold the clock.** *Working placement:* (Manual §24.7.) The clock is also the person's 'form and clock' in the Nara chain. *Live map:* The map is not the disagreeing party here: the QL family manifest holds the clock by techne:m2 and techne:m3 only, while the Deck, Chakra and Sitting are held by studio:nara. *Evidence:* quaternalLogicFamilyManifest.ts:66-74 against 75-100. *Effect on this draft:* Recorded; the hosted Nara instrument's M3 page is the working route.

Conflicts W1, W2, W4 and W5 concern M4 and are in the M4 matrix; W3, the Deck, bears on both and is repeated here.

### 12A.7 Not read

Read in the DLW manual: §§9, 17.10, 19.21, 20.1, 20.3, 22 (all), 24 and 25; the rest of `DLW.md` is unread. Not read: the acceptance walk receipts (the clock's defaults cite 'QL acceptance walk 296'); `EPI-CAPABILITY-READINESS-LEDGER.md` beyond its M3P and M4P rows; the Rust and C sources behind the kernel ops (`m3_reception.rs`, `nara_journey.rs`, `nara_elemental.rs`, `crates/ql-mef`); the hosted Expressions frame beyond file headers; `nara.md` §A2; the content of the authored coordinate Journeys for M3 and M3′ (existence only: `expressions-app/collections/bimba/coordinates/m/M3.journey.json`, `M3-prime.journey.json`); the shell's tests; and whether O-I main `f78bc6aa4` is an ancestor of the env-1 HEAD.

## 14. Coordinate-family refraction

### M3 — Mahāmāyā

Semantic/ontological identity:

- determinative symbolic matter;
- universal transcription;
- 64-fold change/genetic field;
- charge/orientation/narrative/world-form computation;
- #3 totalisation of the 1-2-3 Cosmic pole.

### S3 — Software Factory

Standing technological reflection:

- turns potential/specification into determinate built form;
- supplies the standing production/manufacture reflection of Mahāmāyā's form-giving relation;
- may provide build, graph, generated-artifact or executable-body capabilities used by M3′ without becoming M3 semantic authority.

### S3′ — Chronos

Operative reflection:

- current temporal inscription;
- event/time sequencing and world-clock operation;
- the operative `when / sequence / state transition` face through which M3 determination occurs in running time.

### M3′ — `epi.deep.m3`

Deep compositional/experiential instrument:

- professional symbolic-genetic clock/transcription workspace;
- deterministic address/charge/rotation/transformation inspection;
- Tarot/I-Ching/RNA/codon/clock composition;
- living-rūpa/fold form generated from the same 64/384/472 state;
- same-state human and Agent access through provenance-bearing refs.

Cross-coordinate dependencies remain many-to-many. M3′ uses M1/M2 kernel, M4 protected context, QL formal relations and native O:I product capabilities where required.

## 15. Provenance / decision register

### CURRENT-CANONICAL / ratified system laws

- M3 as Mahāmāyā / dynamic computational matrix / universal transcription.
- 64 as primary M3 address-space.
- A/T/C/G two-bit values and I-Ching values `6/9/8/7`.
- 64 hexagrams / codons and 384 line changes.
- 16 pair field as currently dataset-backed.
- three M3 matrix families and i/j/k axis assignment.
- four-charge `pp/mm/mp/pm` closed form.
- raw 1440 / normalised 360 integral and suit totals.
- 40 non-dual / 24 dual and `40×7 + 24×8 = 472`.
- 56 Minor Arcana exact-cover of 64 codons.
- 22 Major Arcana current core table.
- 360/720 M3 world-clock substrate.
- constitutional `M3/S3/S3′/M3′ = Mahāmāyā/Software Factory/Chronos/epi.deep.m3`.

### CURRENT-AUTHORED-POSITION / human-ratified in this recovery

- M3 is one integrated deterministic form-transcription system.
- Nara→Mahāmāyā re-entry is part of the computation.
- L2′ elemental reading participates in translating whole/entity constitution toward nucleotide form.
- rotational states are Tarot's granularised orientation language beyond upright/reversed.
- Tarot sequences can transcribe and track entity/session/development narratives.
- Nara session intent includes full-deck shuffle/draw trajectory and I-Ching transformation throws.
- Fool/World carry the additional start/stop `0/1` governance roles of the `22+2` relation.
- original 16 static clock lenses remain the primary settled aperture matrix.
- Fibonacci/Pisano is pre-lensic/base rather than a seventeenth static lens.
- exact Spanda `tick/pulse/walk` semantics remain open rather than being fixed by later “walk” terminology.
- the four material elemental carriers continue from M2's `4×18` vibrational field into M3's `4×16` form field.
- polarity×mobility can be embodied directly as fold/rūpa state; three such sites give the existing six-bit/64-state M3 form.
- 64 motifs, 384 one-bit changes and 472 orientations are concomitant form registers of the same Mahāmāyā state.

### IMPLEMENTATION-FACT

- C pair matrices, M3 operators, codon classification, rotational generation/profile, 360 invariant tests and DET quaternion overlay exist.
- C environment quaternion currently determines active rotational state for active codons.
- Rust profile currently computes `q_cosmic` from a selected codon.
- Rust currently maps degree→address64 and has a proportional 12×7 lens-mode→472 projection.
- Nara canonical Tarot artifact currently carries only `upright|reversed`.
- Nara canonical I-Ching artifact carries moving lines/result/nuclear structure and M3 refs.
- current profile bus does not carry the 16-lens M3 stack.
- current profile bus does not carry a typed fold/rūpa form-state.
- current Rust `rna_phase` is a polarity-wide bit toggle, not T→U transcription.

### RESEARCH-PROPOSITION

- T→U 37/27/101 transcription as the complete RNA expressional bridge.
- 80-fold Tarot expression as 56 Minor + 22 Major + Fool/World boundary-governance roles.
- exact relation of three M3 operators to Clifford reversion / grade involution / Clifford conjugation.
- Cl(4,2) / Pauli / spinor interpretation of the M3 matrix field beyond already-landed quaternion-axis assignment.
- α / `137 = 64 + 72 + 1` as psychoid number-form connecting the 1-2-3 architecture with physical/personal coupling registers.
- full K² × T² visual/material realisation of the world clock.
- complete entity-level deterministic inscription from personal/lived quaternion into the M3 field.
- the specific geometric solver/mesh family through which the now-defined fold/rūpa grammar becomes physical/rendered body.

### UNRESOLVED / requires later decision or parity proof

1. canonical `Q_entity/Q_composed → primary address64/codon` transcriber;
2. exact semantic definition of pair `difference_value`, especially class-stable Matrix-2 values;
3. (closed 19 Oct 2026: the `M3_RES_MATRIX` gaps are retired) relation between the 8 72→64 target collisions and 9 source round-trip non-closures;
4. current polarity-wide Rust RNA phase vs T→U transcription architecture;
5. code/spec expression of Fool/World additional governance roles instead of ambiguous “two extra transcendent cards”;
6. full biological/canonical audit of current simplified codon→amino and 24-output tables;
7. 16-lens + Fibonacci-ground fields on the shared profile bus;
8. exact canonical semantic map from 12×7 `(lens,mode)` states into 472 codon-rotation cells; current Rust mapping is proportional;
9. exact Spanda pulse/tick/two-phase-superposition relation and disposition of “walk” terminology;
10. typed M3 fold/rūpa form-state projection on the shared profile bus;
11. exact fold-body geometry/solver preserving all M3 state transformations and reciprocal aperture operation;
12. karyotype/chromosome branch depth and its exact relation to Major Arcana / amino outputs;
13. current live astronomical/world provider evidence for genuine live-now cosmic inscription;
14. Tarot full-session trajectory / shuffle / 7-or-8 orientation payload landing;
15. I-Ching session transformation trajectory integration;
16. correction of the seed claim `Z4 ≅ Z2×Z2`: these groups have equal cardinality but are not group-isomorphic; any two-bit nucleotide feature mapping must be stated as a bijection/encoding or a different algebraic relation, not this group isomorphism.

## 17. Recognition / acceptance for the M3 programme

A future M3 implementation is recognisably Mahāmāyā when all of the following can be demonstrated against one current entity/event/world state:

1. **One address, many lawful faces.** Codon, hexagram, Tarot, rotation, charge, clock and fold/rūpa views co-reference one state rather than being independently recomputed widgets.
2. **Determinative provenance.** The user/Agent can inspect why the current form/address/orientation was selected and which inputs/provider/source relations contributed.
3. **Real Nara re-entry.** A situated entity/event/session can enter the M3 computation without pretending the current missing inverse-transcriber already exists.
4. **Granular Tarot expression.** A card can carry its M3 7/8-state orientation while retaining intelligible upright/reversed coarse polarity.
5. **Generated spatial form.** The current 64 address can render as a six-bit fold/rūpa motif, its six elementary neighbours exactly match the 384 one-bit/line-change graph, and its active 7/8 orientation is the pose of that same form.
6. **Elemental continuity.** Earth/Fire/Water/Air can be traced through the M2 `4×18` source field, M3 `4×16` target field, nucleotide family and form state without renderer-local remapping.
7. **Narrative trajectory.** A Nara session can accumulate a Tarot sequence as an archetypal developmental path and I-Ching throws as a transformation path.
8. **RNA expression is coherent.** DNA/RNA phase follows the ratified transcription law and does not silently apply a contradictory bit transform.
9. **Clock closure is visible.** Four-charge accounting, 360 integral, 720 phase, Fibonacci ground, lenses and current world position can be traced as parts of one computation.
10. **Cymatic relation is preserved.** M2 standing-wave/evidence is visibly upstream of M3 symbolic/form crystallisation rather than copied into M3 as an independent solver.
11. **Lens namespaces stay distinct.** 12×6 MEF/L-L′, 12×7 playing landscape and M3 16 static apertures/Fibonacci ground remain separately named and explicitly related.
12. **Research stays live.** Clifford, α/137, RNA, biological, 16+1 and Spanda developments remain first-class inspectable propositions rather than being erased from the product because they are unfinished.
13. **Human and Agent inhabit the same world.** Both use the same current refs, operations, readiness and provenance rather than separate UI-only and agent-only symbolic reconstructions.
14. **M3 remains one system.** The experience returns the integrated form-computation: harmonic/cymatic potential becoming symbolic matter, spatial rūpa, transformation, expression, narrative, clock/world and situated return.

---

## 18. Return

The recovered Mahāmāyā relation is:

```text
M1
relational/harmonic parent act
        ↓
M2
vibrational · cymatic · elemental world-condition
        ↓
M3
determinative psychoid form transcription
        │
        ├─ 4-state nucleotide / 64-address matter
        ├─ four-charge bioquaternion
        ├─ six-bit fold/rūpa motif
        ├─ I-Ching / 384 change grammar
        ├─ 7/8-fold / 472-state oriented expression
        ├─ DNA→RNA transcription
        ├─ Tarot narrative/archetypal language
        └─ 360°/720° lensic world-clock
        ↓
M4
situated lived person / paśu / event / narrative
        │
        └────────────────────────↺
             returns as something M3 can transcribe
```

This reciprocal circuit is the matrix centre.

Mahāmāyā is the first point at which the `1/2/3` cosmic field becomes a **fully writable and inhabitable form**, and therefore the point at which that same cosmic grammar can later receive a lived individual/event back into itself. Her clock is the totalised cosmic form; her codon is its compressed inscription; her fold/rūpa is its spatial articulation; her I-Ching is its change grammar; her RNA is its expression passage; her Tarot is its archetypal narrative language; her rotational state is the finer orientation through which that same form is actually appearing now.
